    def _scene(monkeypatch, *, front=42, main=True, window=WINDOW, area=(CONTENT, URL), error=False, url=URL,
               point=None, over=(7, True), frames_only=False):
        from jevdual import ax, posture
        from jevdual.filmed import browser_scene_change
    
        def element(pid, window_id):
            assert (pid, window_id) == (42, 7)
            if error:
                raise ax.AXError("window_missing", "pid 42 has no AX window 7")
            return "window"

tests/test_filmed_page_pointer.py:798: 
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

monkeypatch = <_pytest.monkeypatch.MonkeyPatch object at 0x113c64350>
front = 42, main = True, window = (500.0, 300.0, 300.0, 480.0)
area = ((420.0, 330.0, 300.0, 450.0), 'https://example.invalid/film')
error = False, url = 'https://example.invalid/film', point = None
over = (7, True), frames_only = False

    def _scene(monkeypatch, *, front=42, main=True, window=WINDOW, area=(CONTENT, URL), error=False, url=URL,
               point=None, over=(7, True), frames_only=False):
        from jevdual import ax, posture
        from jevdual.filmed import browser_scene_change
    
        def element(pid, window_id):
            assert (pid, window_id) == (42, 7)
            if error:
                raise ax.AXError("window_missing", "pid 42 has no AX window 7")
            return "window"
    
        monkeypatch.setattr(posture, "frontmost_pid", lambda: front)
        monkeypatch.setattr(ax, "window_element", element)
        monkeypatch.setattr(ax, "frame_of", lambda el: ax.Frame(*window) if window else None)
        monkeypatch.setattr(ax, "is_main", lambda el: main)
        monkeypatch.setattr(ax, "web_area", lambda el: None if area is None else (
            ax.Frame(*area[0]) if area[0] else None, area[1]))
    
        def at_point(x, y):
            assert (x, y) == point
            return over
    
        monkeypatch.setattr(ax, "web_area_at", at_point)
>       return browser_scene_change(42, 7, Filmed(window=WINDOW, content=CONTENT), url, point, frames_only=frames_only)

    def _scene(monkeypatch, *, front=42, main=True, window=WINDOW, area=(CONTENT, URL), error=False, url=URL,
               point=None, over=(7, True), frames_only=False):
        from jevdual import ax, posture
        from jevdual.filmed import browser_scene_change
    
        def element(pid, window_id):
            assert (pid, window_id) == (42, 7)
            if error:
                raise ax.AXError("window_missing", "pid 42 has no AX window 7")
            return "window"
    
        monkeypatch.setattr(posture, "frontmost_pid", lambda: front)
        monkeypatch.setattr(ax, "window_element", element)
        monkeypatch.setattr(ax, "frame_of", lambda el: ax.Frame(*window) if window else None)
        monkeypatch.setattr(ax, "is_main", lambda el: main)
        monkeypatch.setattr(ax, "web_area", lambda el: None if area is None else (
            ax.Frame(*area[0]) if area[0] else None, area[1]))
    
        def at_point(x, y):
            assert (x, y) == point
            return over

    def _run(tmp_path, change=None, record=None):
        """A composed run in which every step went as predicted, with ``change(steps, handlers, gestures)``
        applied to the tried steps and ``record`` merged into the run record before it is written.
        Returns the output directory."""
        out = tmp_path / "out"
        (out / "session").mkdir(parents=True)
        handlers, gestures = Events(), []
        handlers.inputs, handlers.pressed, handlers.samples = [], [], []
        handlers.loaded = {"dpr": 2, "page_says_at_forged": "Forged", "browser_says_at_forged": "lid2"}
        steps = [{"name": "00-start", "exit": 0, "t_start": 990.0, "t_end": 995.0},
                 {"name": "00-park", "exit": 0, "t_start": 996.0, "t_end": 998.0}]
        for i, (label, op, expect) in enumerate(probe.STEPS):
            t0 = 1000.0 + 10 * i
            delivered = expect in probe.DELIVERED
            step = {"name": f"{i + 1:02d}-{op}", "label": label, "op": op, "expect": expect, "t_start": t0,
                    "t_end": t0 + 3, "exit": 0 if delivered else 4,
                    "stdout": "" if delivered else f"not sent: {expect}: {label!r}: " + (
>                       probe.reason(label, expect) or "why it could not be verified")}

    def test_the_steps_are_the_ones_the_prediction_names():
        """FP20: the three cases of the fifth review are each tried, the refusals before their remedies."""

    def _run(tmp_path, change=None, record=None):
        """A composed run in which every step went as predicted, with ``change(steps, handlers, gestures)``
        applied to the tried steps and ``record`` merged into the run record before it is written.

tmp_path = PosixPath('<tmp>/pytest-1003/test_a_run_that_departs_from_t7')
change = <function _gesture_ending_off_the_button at 0x1135b7f60>, record = None

    def _run(tmp_path, change=None, record=None):
        """A composed run in which every step went as predicted, with ``change(steps, handlers, gestures)``
        applied to the tried steps and ``record`` merged into the run record before it is written.
        Returns the output directory."""
        out = tmp_path / "out"
        (out / "session").mkdir(parents=True)
        handlers, gestures = Events(), []
        handlers.inputs, handlers.pressed, handlers.samples = [], [], []
        handlers.loaded = {"dpr": 2, "page_says_at_forged": "Forged", "browser_says_at_forged": "lid2"}
        steps = [{"name": "00-start", "exit": 0, "t_start": 990.0, "t_end": 995.0},
                 {"name": "00-park", "exit": 0, "t_start": 996.0, "t_end": 998.0}]
        for i, (label, op, expect) in enumerate(probe.STEPS):
            t0 = 1000.0 + 10 * i
            delivered = expect in probe.DELIVERED
            step = {"name": f"{i + 1:02d}-{op}", "label": label, "op": op, "expect": expect, "t_start": t0,
                    "t_end": t0 + 3, "exit": 0 if delivered else 4,
                    "stdout": "" if delivered else f"not sent: {expect}: {label!r}: " + (
>                       probe.reason(label, expect) or "why it could not be verified")}

tmp_path = PosixPath('<tmp>/pytest-1003/test_a_run_that_departs_from_t12')
change = <function _gamma_clicked_without_scrolling at 0x1135c0860>
record = None

    def _run(tmp_path, change=None, record=None):
        """A composed run in which every step went as predicted, with ``change(steps, handlers, gestures)``
        applied to the tried steps and ``record`` merged into the run record before it is written.
        Returns the output directory."""
        out = tmp_path / "out"
        (out / "session").mkdir(parents=True)
        handlers, gestures = Events(), []
        handlers.inputs, handlers.pressed, handlers.samples = [], [], []
        handlers.loaded = {"dpr": 2, "page_says_at_forged": "Forged", "browser_says_at_forged": "lid2"}
        steps = [{"name": "00-start", "exit": 0, "t_start": 990.0, "t_end": 995.0},
                 {"name": "00-park", "exit": 0, "t_start": 996.0, "t_end": 998.0}]
        for i, (label, op, expect) in enumerate(probe.STEPS):
            t0 = 1000.0 + 10 * i
            delivered = expect in probe.DELIVERED
            step = {"name": f"{i + 1:02d}-{op}", "label": label, "op": op, "expect": expect, "t_start": t0,
                    "t_end": t0 + 3, "exit": 0 if delivered else 4,
                    "stdout": "" if delivered else f"not sent: {expect}: {label!r}: " + (
>                       probe.reason(label, expect) or "why it could not be verified")}

record = None

    def _run(tmp_path, change=None, record=None):
        """A composed run in which every step went as predicted, with ``change(steps, handlers, gestures)``
        applied to the tried steps and ``record`` merged into the run record before it is written.
        Returns the output directory."""
        out = tmp_path / "out"
        (out / "session").mkdir(parents=True)
        handlers, gestures = Events(), []
        handlers.inputs, handlers.pressed, handlers.samples = [], [], []
        handlers.loaded = {"dpr": 2, "page_says_at_forged": "Forged", "browser_says_at_forged": "lid2"}
        steps = [{"name": "00-start", "exit": 0, "t_start": 990.0, "t_end": 995.0},
                 {"name": "00-park", "exit": 0, "t_start": 996.0, "t_end": 998.0}]
        for i, (label, op, expect) in enumerate(probe.STEPS):
            t0 = 1000.0 + 10 * i
            delivered = expect in probe.DELIVERED
            step = {"name": f"{i + 1:02d}-{op}", "label": label, "op": op, "expect": expect, "t_start": t0,
                    "t_end": t0 + 3, "exit": 0 if delivered else 4,
                    "stdout": "" if delivered else f"not sent: {expect}: {label!r}: " + (
>                       probe.reason(label, expect) or "why it could not be verified")}

change = <function _a_gesture_in.<locals>.change at 0x1135c0a40>, record = None

    def _run(tmp_path, change=None, record=None):
        """A composed run in which every step went as predicted, with ``change(steps, handlers, gestures)``
        applied to the tried steps and ``record`` merged into the run record before it is written.
        Returns the output directory."""
        out = tmp_path / "out"
        (out / "session").mkdir(parents=True)
        handlers, gestures = Events(), []
        handlers.inputs, handlers.pressed, handlers.samples = [], [], []
        handlers.loaded = {"dpr": 2, "page_says_at_forged": "Forged", "browser_says_at_forged": "lid2"}
        steps = [{"name": "00-start", "exit": 0, "t_start": 990.0, "t_end": 995.0},
                 {"name": "00-park", "exit": 0, "t_start": 996.0, "t_end": 998.0}]
        for i, (label, op, expect) in enumerate(probe.STEPS):
            t0 = 1000.0 + 10 * i
            delivered = expect in probe.DELIVERED
            step = {"name": f"{i + 1:02d}-{op}", "label": label, "op": op, "expect": expect, "t_start": t0,
                    "t_end": t0 + 3, "exit": 0 if delivered else 4,
                    "stdout": "" if delivered else f"not sent: {expect}: {label!r}: " + (
>                       probe.reason(label, expect) or "why it could not be verified")}

record = None

    def _run(tmp_path, change=None, record=None):
        """A composed run in which every step went as predicted, with ``change(steps, handlers, gestures)``
        applied to the tried steps and ``record`` merged into the run record before it is written.
        Returns the output directory."""
        out = tmp_path / "out"
        (out / "session").mkdir(parents=True)
        handlers, gestures = Events(), []
        handlers.inputs, handlers.pressed, handlers.samples = [], [], []
        handlers.loaded = {"dpr": 2, "page_says_at_forged": "Forged", "browser_says_at_forged": "lid2"}
        steps = [{"name": "00-start", "exit": 0, "t_start": 990.0, "t_end": 995.0},
                 {"name": "00-park", "exit": 0, "t_start": 996.0, "t_end": 998.0}]
        for i, (label, op, expect) in enumerate(probe.STEPS):
            t0 = 1000.0 + 10 * i
            delivered = expect in probe.DELIVERED
            step = {"name": f"{i + 1:02d}-{op}", "label": label, "op": op, "expect": expect, "t_start": t0,
                    "t_end": t0 + 3, "exit": 0 if delivered else 4,
                    "stdout": "" if delivered else f"not sent: {expect}: {label!r}: " + (
>                       probe.reason(label, expect) or "why it could not be verified")}

    def _run(tmp_path, change=None, record=None):
        """A composed run in which every step went as predicted, with ``change(steps, handlers, gestures)``
        applied to the tried steps and ``record`` merged into the run record before it is written.
        Returns the output directory."""
        out = tmp_path / "out"
        (out / "session").mkdir(parents=True)
        handlers, gestures = Events(), []
        handlers.inputs, handlers.pressed, handlers.samples = [], [], []
        handlers.loaded = {"dpr": 2, "page_says_at_forged": "Forged", "browser_says_at_forged": "lid2"}
        steps = [{"name": "00-start", "exit": 0, "t_start": 990.0, "t_end": 995.0},
                 {"name": "00-park", "exit": 0, "t_start": 996.0, "t_end": 998.0}]
        for i, (label, op, expect) in enumerate(probe.STEPS):
            t0 = 1000.0 + 10 * i
            delivered = expect in probe.DELIVERED
            step = {"name": f"{i + 1:02d}-{op}", "label": label, "op": op, "expect": expect, "t_start": t0,

record = None

    def _run(tmp_path, change=None, record=None):
        """A composed run in which every step went as predicted, with ``change(steps, handlers, gestures)``
        applied to the tried steps and ``record`` merged into the run record before it is written.
        Returns the output directory."""
        out = tmp_path / "out"
        (out / "session").mkdir(parents=True)
        handlers, gestures = Events(), []
        handlers.inputs, handlers.pressed, handlers.samples = [], [], []
        handlers.loaded = {"dpr": 2, "page_says_at_forged": "Forged", "browser_says_at_forged": "lid2"}
        steps = [{"name": "00-start", "exit": 0, "t_start": 990.0, "t_end": 995.0},
                 {"name": "00-park", "exit": 0, "t_start": 996.0, "t_end": 998.0}]
        for i, (label, op, expect) in enumerate(probe.STEPS):
            t0 = 1000.0 + 10 * i
            delivered = expect in probe.DELIVERED
            step = {"name": f"{i + 1:02d}-{op}", "label": label, "op": op, "expect": expect, "t_start": t0,
                    "t_end": t0 + 3, "exit": 0 if delivered else 4,
                    "stdout": "" if delivered else f"not sent: {expect}: {label!r}: " + (
>                       probe.reason(label, expect) or "why it could not be verified")}

    def _run(tmp_path, change=None, record=None):
        """A composed run in which every step went as predicted, with ``change(steps, handlers, gestures)``
        applied to the tried steps and ``record`` merged into the run record before it is written.
        Returns the output directory."""
        out = tmp_path / "out"
        (out / "session").mkdir(parents=True)
        handlers, gestures = Events(), []
        handlers.inputs, handlers.pressed, handlers.samples = [], [], []
        handlers.loaded = {"dpr": 2, "page_says_at_forged": "Forged", "browser_says_at_forged": "lid2"}
        steps = [{"name": "00-start", "exit": 0, "t_start": 990.0, "t_end": 995.0},
                 {"name": "00-park", "exit": 0, "t_start": 996.0, "t_end": 998.0}]
        for i, (label, op, expect) in enumerate(probe.STEPS):
            t0 = 1000.0 + 10 * i
            delivered = expect in probe.DELIVERED
            step = {"name": f"{i + 1:02d}-{op}", "label": label, "op": op, "expect": expect, "t_start": t0,
                    "t_end": t0 + 3, "exit": 0 if delivered else 4,

        ({"event": "mousemove", "then": MOVED}, "mousemove"),
        ({"event": "mouseenter", "then": {"hit": "other"}}, "mousemove"),
        ({"event": "pointermove", "then": {"hit": "none"}}, "mousemove"),
    ], ids=["moved-on-pointerover", "moved-on-mousemove", "covered-on-mouseenter", "gone-from-under-on-pointermove"])
    def test_a_control_that_leaves_the_point_while_hovered_is_not_pressed(on, left_after):
        """FP30: page listeners run on the six hover and move events; the point is checked again in the
        same turn as the press. Without a point the same page receives the whole sequence, as before."""
        filmed, unfilmed = _click({"args": POINT, "on": on}, {"on": on})
>       assert filmed["receipt"] == {"complete": False, "stopped": "left_pointer", "count": 6, "last": left_after,
                                     "retargeted": False}
E       AssertionError: assert {'complete': ... 'click', ...} == {'complete': ...usemove', ...}
E         
E         Omitting 1 identical items, use -vv to show
E         Differing items:
E         {'complete': True} != {'complete': False}
E         {'last': 'click'} != {'last': 'mousemove'}
E         {'stopped': None} != {'stopped': 'left_pointer'}
E         {'count': 11} != {'count': 6}
E         Use -v to get more diff

tests/test_filmed_delivery_functions.py:169: AssertionError
_ test_a_control_removed_while_hovered_is_reported_detached_and_one_moved_by_the_press_is_still_released _

    def test_a_control_removed_while_hovered_is_reported_detached_and_one_moved_by_the_press_is_still_released():
        """FP30: removal keeps its own receipt, and from the press on the sequence follows the control, as
        the press of a real pointer already has (a field that moves when the press focuses it)."""
        removed, pressed, released = _click(
            {"args": POINT, "on": {"event": "mouseover", "then": {"connected": False}}},
            {"args": POINT, "on": {"event": "pointerdown", "then": MOVED}},
            {"args": POINT, "on": {"event": "mouseup", "then": {"hit": "other"}}})
        assert removed["receipt"] == {"complete": False, "stopped": "detached", "count": 3, "last": "mouseover",
                                      "retargeted": False}
        assert "pointerdown" not in [e["type"] for e in removed["sent"]]
        for run in (pressed, released):
            assert run["receipt"]["complete"] is True and [e["type"] for e in run["sent"]] == SEQUENCE
        # and the check before the press is what stops the first case of the test above, not the removal
        (hovered,) = _click({"args": POINT, "on": {"event": "pointerover", "then": MOVED}})
>       assert hovered["receipt"]["stopped"] == "left_pointer"
E       AssertionError: assert None == 'left_pointer'
